\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{exd5}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{exd5} (\texttt{e6d5}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#1703} (ply 16) [structural distance $d$=46, QAP obj=175]: the master played \texttt{cxd5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pe3} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pc4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{n}f3 as their \emph{B}f1, our \emph{p}b2 as their \emph{N}g1; transferred move \texttt{e6d5} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#1704} (ply 16) [structural distance $d$=46, QAP obj=175]: the master played \texttt{cxd5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pe3} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pc4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{n}f3 as their \emph{B}f1, our \emph{p}b2 as their \emph{N}g1; transferred move \texttt{e6d5} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0003\#2850} (ply 16) [structural distance $d$=45, QAP obj=172]: the master played \texttt{cxd5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pe3} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pc4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{Q}d8 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{B}c8 as their \emph{N}b1; transferred move \texttt{e6d5} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 30 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{exd5} (summed QAP objective 2947, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
